# normalizedDVR_roos_augmentation

## Reason

The previous statement forgot the finite coefficient action in its level comparison.
Successor compatibility of the eU in D(R) does not determine the zero nullhomotopy
needed by the augmentation. Over a coefficient DVR R, take the standard tower
B_j direct-sum B_j[1], B_j=R/(pi^j), and modify only eU_0 by the unipotent
Bockstein B_1 -> B_1[1]. Precomposition with B_2 -> B_1 kills that Bockstein,
so every heU remains true. The limit projection from R direct-sum R[1] is unchanged,
but the asserted cone augmentation acquires a nonzero off-diagonal Bockstein.
Naturality with the two summand idempotents forces every natural coefficient
comparison at this limit to be diagonal, so the original conclusion fails.

The repair retains the finite coefficient action at the selected positive level:
a finite complex N, a chain isomorphism iN from the selected U level to its
restriction, an isomorphism eN in D(B_n) to T.M n, and the factorization equation hN.
This fixes the zero nullhomotopy without assuming fullness of derived restriction.
The canonical strict model already has this data. No target statement or definition
used by a target changes. The intermediate bounded-free statement receives the same
arguments; the adic-cone theorem retains its original statement and supplies them.


## Old

```lean
theorem normalizedDVR_roos_augmentation
    (T : NormalizedDVRTower l E X D hl) (n : ℕ)
    (adj : scalarReductionDerivedExtension l E X n ⊣
      scalarReductionDerivedRestriction l E X n)
    [HasLimitsOfShape (Discrete ℕ) (completedIntegralDerivedCategory l E X D hl)]
    (U : ℕᵒᵖ ⥤ CochainComplex (completedIntegralModuleSheaves l E X D hl) ℤ)
    (hU : CochainComplex.IsNormalizedAdicTower (normalizedDVRUniformizer l E X) U)
    (eU : ∀ j : ℕ, DerivedCategory.Q.obj (U.obj (op (j + 1))) ≅
      (normalizedDVRTowerEvaluation l E X D hl j).obj T.tower)
    (heU : ∀ j : ℕ,
      DerivedCategory.Q.map (U.map (homOfLE (Nat.succ_le_succ j.le_succ)).op) ≫
          (eU j).hom = (eU (j + 1)).hom ≫
        (normalizedDVRTowerTransition l E X D hl j.le_succ).app T.tower) :
    ∃ A : scalarReductionDerivedExtension l E X n ⋙
          scalarReductionDerivedRestriction l E X n ≅
        CochainComplex.LinearScalarCone.derived
          (A := completedIntegralModuleSheaves l E X D hl)
          (normalizedDVRUniformizer l E X ^ (n + 1)),
      let c := CategoryTheory.Pretriangulated.homotopyLimitCone (U ⋙ DerivedCategory.Q)
      let k := (CategoryTheory.Pretriangulated.addOpFunctor (n + 1)).obj (op 0)
      (scalarReductionDerivedRestriction l E X n).map
          ((adj.homEquiv c.pt (T.M n)).symm
            (c.π.app (op (n + 1)) ≫ (eU n).hom ≫ (T.evaluationIso n).hom)) =
        A.hom.app c.pt ≫
          (CochainComplex.LinearScalarCone.derived
            (normalizedDVRUniformizer l E X ^ (n + 1))).map (c.π.app k) ≫
          CochainComplex.LinearScalarCone.derivedAugmentation hU (n + 1) (op 0) ≫
          (eU n).hom ≫
          (T.evaluationIso n).hom
```

```lean
theorem normalizedDVR_bounded_free_cone
    (T : NormalizedDVRTower l E X D hl)
    (M : completedIntegralDerivedCategory l E X D hl)
    [HasProduct (fun n : ℕ ↦ (normalizedDVRTowerEvaluation l E X D hl n).obj T.tower)]
    (p : M ⟶ ∏ᶜ fun n : ℕ ↦ (normalizedDVRTowerEvaluation l E X D hl n).obj T.tower)
    (δ : (∏ᶜ fun n : ℕ ↦ (normalizedDVRTowerEvaluation l E X D hl n).obj T.tower) ⟶
      M⟦(1 : ℤ)⟧)
    (hc : IsDerivedLimitTriangle
      (fun n : ℕ ↦ (normalizedDVRTowerTransition l E X D hl n.le_succ).app T.tower)
      M p δ) (n : ℕ)
    (adj : scalarReductionDerivedExtension l E X n ⊣
      scalarReductionDerivedRestriction l E X n)
    [HasLimitsOfShape (Discrete ℕ) (completedIntegralDerivedCategory l E X D hl)]
    (U : ℕᵒᵖ ⥤ CochainComplex (completedIntegralModuleSheaves l E X D hl) ℤ)
    (hU : CochainComplex.IsNormalizedAdicTower (normalizedDVRUniformizer l E X) U)
    (eU : ∀ j : ℕ, DerivedCategory.Q.obj (U.obj (op (j + 1))) ≅
      (normalizedDVRTowerEvaluation l E X D hl j).obj T.tower)
    (heU : ∀ j : ℕ,
      DerivedCategory.Q.map (U.map (homOfLE (Nat.succ_le_succ j.le_succ)).op) ≫
          (eU j).hom = (eU (j + 1)).hom ≫
        (normalizedDVRTowerTransition l E X D hl j.le_succ).app T.tower) :
    ∃ (c : Cone (CochainComplex.IsNormalizedAdicTower.arrowTower
      (ϖ := normalizedDVRUniformizer l E X) (T := U) (n + 1) ⋙
        CochainComplex.ConeCoimage.cone ⋙ DerivedCategory.Q)),
    CategoryTheory.Pretriangulated.IsHomotopyLimit c ∧
    ∃ (a : (scalarReductionDerivedRestriction l E X n).obj
          ((scalarReductionDerivedExtension l E X n).obj M) ≅ c.pt),
      (scalarReductionDerivedRestriction l E X n).map
          ((adj.homEquiv M (T.M n)).symm
            (p ≫ Pi.π _ n ≫ (T.evaluationIso n).hom)) =
        a.hom ≫ c.π.app (op 0) ≫ DerivedCategory.Q.map
          (CochainComplex.ConeCoimage.ρ.app _ ≫ CochainComplex.ConeCoimage.desc.app _ ≫
            hU.cokerDesc (n + 1) (op 0)) ≫ (eU n).hom ≫
            (T.evaluationIso n).hom
```

## New

```lean
theorem normalizedDVR_roos_augmentation
    (T : NormalizedDVRTower l E X D hl) (n : ℕ)
    (adj : scalarReductionDerivedExtension l E X n ⊣
      scalarReductionDerivedRestriction l E X n)
    [HasLimitsOfShape (Discrete ℕ) (completedIntegralDerivedCategory l E X D hl)]
    (U : ℕᵒᵖ ⥤ CochainComplex (completedIntegralModuleSheaves l E X D hl) ℤ)
    (hU : CochainComplex.IsNormalizedAdicTower (normalizedDVRUniformizer l E X) U)
    (eU : ∀ j : ℕ, DerivedCategory.Q.obj (U.obj (op (j + 1))) ≅
      (normalizedDVRTowerEvaluation l E X D hl j).obj T.tower)
    (_heU : ∀ j : ℕ,
      DerivedCategory.Q.map (U.map (homOfLE (Nat.succ_le_succ j.le_succ)).op) ≫
          (eU j).hom = (eU (j + 1)).hom ≫
        (normalizedDVRTowerTransition l E X D hl j.le_succ).app T.tower)
    (N : CochainComplex (normalizedDVRModules l E X n) ℤ)
    (iN : U.obj (op (n + 1)) ≅
      ((completedIntegralRestrictScalars l E X D hl n).mapHomologicalComplex
        (.up ℤ)).obj N)
    (eN : (@DerivedCategory.Q _ _ _ (HasDerivedCategory.standard _)).obj N ≅ T.M n)
    (hN : (eU n).hom ≫ (T.evaluationIso n).hom =
      DerivedCategory.Q.map iN.hom ≫
        (completedIntegralRestrictScalars l E X D hl n).mapDerivedCategoryFactors.inv.app N ≫
        (normalizedDVRResCompleted l E X D hl n).map eN.hom) :
    ∃ A : scalarReductionDerivedExtension l E X n ⋙
          scalarReductionDerivedRestriction l E X n ≅
        CochainComplex.LinearScalarCone.derived
          (A := completedIntegralModuleSheaves l E X D hl)
          (normalizedDVRUniformizer l E X ^ (n + 1)),
      let c := CategoryTheory.Pretriangulated.homotopyLimitCone (U ⋙ DerivedCategory.Q)
      let k := (CategoryTheory.Pretriangulated.addOpFunctor (n + 1)).obj (op 0)
      (scalarReductionDerivedRestriction l E X n).map
          ((adj.homEquiv c.pt (T.M n)).symm
            (c.π.app (op (n + 1)) ≫ (eU n).hom ≫ (T.evaluationIso n).hom)) =
        A.hom.app c.pt ≫
          (CochainComplex.LinearScalarCone.derived
            (normalizedDVRUniformizer l E X ^ (n + 1))).map (c.π.app k) ≫
          CochainComplex.LinearScalarCone.derivedAugmentation hU (n + 1) (op 0) ≫
          (eU n).hom ≫
          (T.evaluationIso n).hom
```

```lean
theorem normalizedDVR_bounded_free_cone
    (T : NormalizedDVRTower l E X D hl)
    (M : completedIntegralDerivedCategory l E X D hl)
    [HasProduct (fun n : ℕ ↦ (normalizedDVRTowerEvaluation l E X D hl n).obj T.tower)]
    (p : M ⟶ ∏ᶜ fun n : ℕ ↦ (normalizedDVRTowerEvaluation l E X D hl n).obj T.tower)
    (δ : (∏ᶜ fun n : ℕ ↦ (normalizedDVRTowerEvaluation l E X D hl n).obj T.tower) ⟶
      M⟦(1 : ℤ)⟧)
    (hc : IsDerivedLimitTriangle
      (fun n : ℕ ↦ (normalizedDVRTowerTransition l E X D hl n.le_succ).app T.tower)
      M p δ) (n : ℕ)
    (adj : scalarReductionDerivedExtension l E X n ⊣
      scalarReductionDerivedRestriction l E X n)
    [HasLimitsOfShape (Discrete ℕ) (completedIntegralDerivedCategory l E X D hl)]
    (U : ℕᵒᵖ ⥤ CochainComplex (completedIntegralModuleSheaves l E X D hl) ℤ)
    (hU : CochainComplex.IsNormalizedAdicTower (normalizedDVRUniformizer l E X) U)
    (eU : ∀ j : ℕ, DerivedCategory.Q.obj (U.obj (op (j + 1))) ≅
      (normalizedDVRTowerEvaluation l E X D hl j).obj T.tower)
    (heU : ∀ j : ℕ,
      DerivedCategory.Q.map (U.map (homOfLE (Nat.succ_le_succ j.le_succ)).op) ≫
          (eU j).hom = (eU (j + 1)).hom ≫
        (normalizedDVRTowerTransition l E X D hl j.le_succ).app T.tower)
    (N : CochainComplex (normalizedDVRModules l E X n) ℤ)
    (iN : U.obj (op (n + 1)) ≅
      ((completedIntegralRestrictScalars l E X D hl n).mapHomologicalComplex
        (.up ℤ)).obj N)
    (eN : (@DerivedCategory.Q _ _ _ (HasDerivedCategory.standard _)).obj N ≅ T.M n)
    (hN : (eU n).hom ≫ (T.evaluationIso n).hom =
      DerivedCategory.Q.map iN.hom ≫
        (completedIntegralRestrictScalars l E X D hl n).mapDerivedCategoryFactors.inv.app N ≫
        (normalizedDVRResCompleted l E X D hl n).map eN.hom) :
    ∃ (c : Cone (CochainComplex.IsNormalizedAdicTower.arrowTower
      (ϖ := normalizedDVRUniformizer l E X) (T := U) (n + 1) ⋙
        CochainComplex.ConeCoimage.cone ⋙ DerivedCategory.Q)),
    CategoryTheory.Pretriangulated.IsHomotopyLimit c ∧
    ∃ (a : (scalarReductionDerivedRestriction l E X n).obj
          ((scalarReductionDerivedExtension l E X n).obj M) ≅ c.pt),
      (scalarReductionDerivedRestriction l E X n).map
          ((adj.homEquiv M (T.M n)).symm
            (p ≫ Pi.π _ n ≫ (T.evaluationIso n).hom)) =
        a.hom ≫ c.π.app (op 0) ≫ DerivedCategory.Q.map
          (CochainComplex.ConeCoimage.ρ.app _ ≫ CochainComplex.ConeCoimage.desc.app _ ≫
            hU.cokerDesc (n + 1) (op 0)) ≫ (eU n).hom ≫
            (T.evaluationIso n).hom
```
